- **PARALLEL-AUDIT**: Platform-neutral definitions for delegating independent audit slices. - **parallel-reviewer**: 你是一个代码审查子任务执行者。你会收到一组文件进行审查。 ([diguike/book-claude-skill](https://github.com/diguike/book-claude-skill/blob/HEAD/skills/code-review-snapshots/v9-subagent/agents/parallel-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **parallel-reviewer**: Run four specialized reviewers in parallel and consolidate findings ([dowdiness/skills](https://github.com/dowdiness/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/parallel-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **parallel-reviewer**: &gt; **Evolution of the existing `reviewer` agent.** Spawns four specialised reviewers in parallel, consolidates findings, and returns a single verdict. Roughly 3-5× faster than the sequential reviewer and uses ~4× less main-context. ([hamzaPixl/nuva-hhm-workshop](https://github.com/hamzaPixl/nuva-hhm-workshop/blob/HEAD/artifacts/agents/parallel-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **parallel-reviewer**: Parallel code review using 4 specialist agents (typescript-reviewer, security-analyzer, testing-reviewer, verification-runner). - **parallel-reviewer**: Coordinates 5-agent parallel code review. Spawns react-reviewer, security-analyzer, testing-reviewer, verification-runner, and uiux-designer. Deduplicates findings via context-supervisor. ([surfertas/claude-react-rtk](https://github.com/surfertas/claude-react-rtk/blob/HEAD/plugins/react-rtk/agents/parallel-reviewer.md))
- **parallel-reviewer**: 作業中の変更を並行してレビューする批判的レビュアー。コード品質・バグ/エッジケース・ユーザー意図とのズレを重点的にチェックする。実装の節目（関数・モジュール完成時、機能の一区切り、コミット前、PR作成前）に積極的（proactively）に呼び出すこと。実装担当エージェントが「自分のコードは正しい」と思い込んでいる前提で、あえて反対側から疑う。 ([theMistletoe/cloudflare-templete](https://github.com/theMistletoe/cloudflare-templete/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/parallel-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **parallel-reviewer**: Parallel code review using 4 specialist agents (python-reviewer, security-analyzer, testing-reviewer, verification-runner). Use for thorough review of significant changes. ([victorigualada/claude-home-assistant-plugin](https://github.com/victorigualada/claude-home-assistant-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/parallel-reviewer.md))
- **parallel-rounds**: This is a vendor-neutral control plane for several isolated coding agents. It coordinates work; it does not change architecture, backlog authority, priorities, or acceptance bars. `docs/architecture.md`, `docs/todo.md`, linked detail sections, source, and approved deviations remain authoritative. ([TKontu/dev-framework](https://github.com/TKontu/dev-framework/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/parallel-rounds.md))
- **parallel-runner**: Executes multiple simple tasks in parallel. Use when you need to run several independent operations simultaneously (searches, file operations, verifications). NOT for complex or dependent tasks. ([hnkatze/claude-rules-content](https://github.com/hnkatze/claude-rules-content/blob/HEAD/packs/developer-tools/agents/parallel-runner.md))
- **parallel-runner**: Isolated worker for an independent unit of work that can run in parallel with other work in the main session. Use when the main agent needs to delegate a self-contained task (write a test, scaffold a file, run a long command) without context-switching. Has full tool access in its own context window. ([shaunchew/savvy-template](https://github.com/shaunchew/savvy-template/blob/HEAD/agents/parallel-runner.md) · 2 copies)
- **parallel-safety-reviewer**: Read-only specialist that evaluates changed MSTest tests and configuration for cross-test parallel-safety, resource declaration mismatches, and avoidable serialization. ([microsoft/testfx](https://github.com/microsoft/testfx/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/parallel-safety-reviewer.agent.md) · ★1.1k)
- **parallel-search**: Low-level paired-search primitive. Given ONE query, reads Google through formal LCU MCP and runs WebSearch with the same query, then returns the raw result sets side by side without synthesis or deduplication. Use when one query genuinely needs both engines. Do not use for broad fan-out research or browser diagnostics. ([GGGODLIN/gggodlin-cc-harness](https://github.com/GGGODLIN/gggodlin-cc-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/parallel-search.md) · ★74)

Parallel заметно быстрее, но на неоднозначных русскоязычных названиях чаще возвращает другой объект. Переходить на него как на единственный источник поиска по результатам этой пробы не стоит; перспективен `advanced` с короткими точными запросами и проверк ([softmg/otgolosok](https://github.com/softmg/otgolosok/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/parallel-search-probe.md) · ★3)

Read-only on the target code; returns a structured findings summary. Not for use outside a bug hunt. Give it the work units inline (id, title, kind build|apply|wire|verify, seeds, depends_on, optional size) plus the ledger or scratch dir to write to. It runs the partition script, strikes false hits, clusters with judgment, and returns a short schedule - layers, parallel clusters, WU ids, commit tags, file counts, token estimates, warnings, and whether one agent is enough. Use it for work that spans many files or areas ("build X, then apply it everywhere"). Do NOT use it to edit source or run builds. - **pass-00-scoping**: Pass 0 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Scoping & Repo Map. Builds the machine-readable codebase map every other pass consumes instead of re-discovering it. - **pass-01-injection**: Pass 1 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Injection & Untrusted Input. Traces every external value from entry point to sink and flags paths reaching a sink without validation or context-appropriate encoding. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/01-injection.md) · ★1)
- **pass-02-authentication**: Pass 2 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Authentication. Audits how identity is established and maintained — session handling, credential verification, token lifecycle — never who may do what. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/02-authentication.md) · ★1)
- **pass-03-authorization**: Pass 3 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Authorization & IDOR. Assumes authentication works and audits only who may do what to which resource. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/03-authorization.md) · ★1)
- **pass-04-secrets**: Pass 4 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Secrets & Sensitive Data Exposure. Finds every place a secret or sensitive datum is exposed to someone who shouldn't see it. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/04-secrets.md) · ★1)
- **pass-05-error-handling**: Pass 5 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Error Handling. Verifies every failure path is handled explicitly and produces a state the caller can act on, with nothing silently swallowed. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/05-error-handling.md) · ★1)
- **pass-06-concurrency**: Pass 6 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Concurrency. Finds races, unsafe shared state, and interleavings that corrupt data under concurrent execution. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/06-concurrency.md) · ★1)
- **pass-07-resource-lifecycle**: Pass 7 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Resource Lifecycle. Proves every acquired resource is released on every exit path, including error paths. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/07-resource-lifecycle.md) · ★1)
- **pass-08-data-access**: Pass 8 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Data Access Patterns. Finds data-access patterns that are correct at small scale but become a bottleneck as data grows — N+1 queries, missing indexes, unbounded reads. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/08-data-access.md) · ★1)
- **pass-09-complexity**: Pass 9 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Complexity & Algorithmic Cost. Finds algorithmic complexity that will not survive the scale the system is expected to reach, with a stated breaking point for every finding. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/09-complexity.md) · ★1)
- **pass-10-memory-growth**: Pass 10 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Memory Growth. Finds unbounded memory growth over the life of a long-running process. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/10-memory-growth.md) · ★1)
- **pass-11-external-calls**: Pass 11 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — External Calls, Timeouts & Resilience. Proves no single slow or failing dependency can take the whole service down. Also runs in Level 0 triage. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/11-external-calls.md) · ★1)
- **pass-12-idempotency**: Pass 12 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Idempotency. Determines whether operations that can be invoked more than once produce a duplicated side effect. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/12-idempotency.md) · ★1)
- **pass-13-transactions**: Pass 13 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Transactions. Verifies operations that must be atomic actually are, via a real transaction or an equivalent compensating mechanism. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/13-transactions.md) · ★1)
- **pass-14-config-hardening**: Pass 14 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Configuration & Environment Hardening. Finds environment/config-level exposure — debug surfaces reachable in prod, permissive defaults, under-hardened infrastructure. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/14-config-hardening.md) · ★1)
- **pass-15-supply-chain**: Pass 15 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Supply Chain & Dependencies. Judges whether the dependency and build pipeline can be trusted — pinning discipline, install-time risk, CI trust boundaries. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/15-supply-chain.md) · ★1)
- **pass-16-logging-observability**: Pass 16 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Logging & Observability. Verifies the system produces enough signal to diagnose an incident without leaking sensitive data or drowning in noise. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/16-logging-observability.md) · ★1)
- **pass-17-api-contracts**: Pass 17 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — API Contracts. Verifies the API's actual behavior matches its declared contract so callers can rely on it without reading the implementation. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/17-api-contracts.md) · ★1)
- **pass-18-cross-module**: Pass 18 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Cross-Module Contracts. Verifies both sides of every module/service trust boundary agree on the contract; runs after the analysis passes, before Pass 20. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/18-cross-module.md) · ★1)
- **pass-19-test-gaps**: Pass 19 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Test Gaps. Finds where test coverage is absent or misleading for code that matters, not just a raw coverage percentage. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/19-test-gaps.md) · ★1)
- **pass-20-verification**: Pass 20 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Verification & False-Positive Filter. Relocates, falsifies, and conservatively re-scores every other pass's findings. Always runs last; no finding reaches a human without passing through it. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/20-verification.md) · ★1)
- **pass-21-rate-limiting-abuse**: Pass 21 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Rate Limiting & Abuse. Proves no entry point permits unbounded automated abuse — brute force, enumeration, resource exhaustion, business-logic abuse. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/21-rate-limiting-abuse.md) · ★1)
- **pass-22-cryptography**: Pass 22 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Cryptography. Finds every cryptographic primitive, key, or random value chosen or used incorrectly enough to break its guarantee. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/22-cryptography.md) · ★1)
- **pass-23-migration-safety**: Pass 23 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Schema Migration Safety. Verifies schema migrations are safe to run live — reversible or explicitly two-phase, with no unbounded write-blocking locks. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/23-migration-safety.md) · ★1)
- **pass-24-compliance**: Pass 24 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Compliance & Data Lifecycle. Verifies regulated data's full lifecycle — collection, storage, access, retention, erasure — is actually implementable end to end. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/24-compliance.md) · ★1)
- **pass-25-ai-feature-security**: Pass 25 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — AI Feature Security (conditional, OWASP LLM Top 10 aligned). Runs only when Pass 0 detects LLM/agent features; otherwise writes a single skip line. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/25-ai-feature-security.md) · ★1)
- **pass-abstraction**: Abstraction review pass — are the functions, types, interfaces and parameters this change introduces or reshapes drawn at the right level for their job? Mixed levels, leaky or missing abstractions, speculative generality, contracts that do not say what they guarantee. Capped at P2. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-abstraction.md) · ★9)
- **pass-ask-human**: Ask-your-human review pass — added on demand when the session suspects a question whose answer lives outside the repo. Every finding is a question addressed to the PR author's AI implementer, telling it to put the question to a person rather than answer it itself. It may block only when it names the failure under the bad answer. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-ask-human.md) · ★9)
- **pass-backcompat**: Backward-compatibility review pass — what breaks in the deploy window, when old and new code run together and old clients, in-flight messages, and persisted state are still live? Findings name the protection. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-backcompat.md) · ★9)
- **pass-baseline**: Always-on review pass — claimed invariants, correctness, tests, conventions, sibling PRs. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-baseline.md) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **pass-behaviour-delta**: Base-versus-head behaviour on every modified, rewrapped, replaced or deleted line of non-test code — what the old code did on the paths the PR did not set out to change, and whether the new code still does it. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-behaviour-delta.md) · ★9)
- **pass-bounds**: Resource and trust bounds pass (sub-lens of security) — loops whose exit another party controls, size checks after allocation, unvalidated remote data, unbounded growth, internal facts leaking out. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-bounds.md) · ★9)
- **pass-by-reference**: Architectural principles for zero-waste agent communication, fast relays, and emergency bypass queues. ([lew42/monorepo](https://github.com/lew42/monorepo/blob/HEAD/public/framework/ai/doc/agents/pass-by-reference.md) · ★1)
- **pass-claims**: Claim-check pass (sub-lens of intent) — for each checkable claim in the PR title or body (fixes a warning, supports a version, prevents a failure), verifies that the diff actually makes it true, everywhere it applies. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-claims.md) · ★9)
- **pass-concurrency**: Concurrency review pass — planned when the diff touches synchronisation or a read-then-write shape, or added when the session suspects a race. Every finding names what races, walks the interleaving step by step, assesses how often it can really happen, and names the lock that fits. Falsifies its own findings first; full severity. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-concurrency.md) · ★9)
- **pass-consistency**: Consistency review pass — does the change treat alike what is alike, within itself and against the code around it? A sibling left out, one path handled differently, a pattern the repo follows everywhere but here. Inconsistency is where bugs hide; findings name both sides. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-consistency.md) · ★9)
- **pass-contracts**: External-contracts review pass — provider payloads verified field-by-field, acceptance semantics, caveat owners. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-contracts.md) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **pass-data-safety**: Data-safety review pass — migrations DDL, blast radius, backfills; carries the migration gate. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-data-safety.md) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **pass-deferred**: Deferred-work review pass (sub-lens of concurrency) — when work moves onto a queue, loop, timer or batch, checks who wakes it, who reads before it drains, and what coalescing merges. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-deferred.md) · ★9)
- **pass-delivery**: Delivery-semantics review pass — at-least-once handlers, redelivery, drop paths, fetch windows. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-delivery.md) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **pass-duplication**: Duplication review pass — does the change write the same knowledge twice, or again where the repo already has it? Both sites cited, and what drifts when one changes without the other. Capped at P2. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-duplication.md) · ★9)
- **pass-engineer**: Compiler pass specialist for the lancy backend. Use when writing or reviewing analyses (CFG, liveness, dominance, reverse-post-order), transforms (SSA destruction, ABI lowering, legalization, pseudo cleanup, prologue/epilogue insertion), or when changing the pass pipeline ordering. Spawn proactively when a task involves pass ordering decisions, IR invariants, or cross-pass contracts. ([fexolm/lancy](https://github.com/fexolm/lancy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pass-engineer.md))
- **pass-graph-pipeline**: Trace pass divergence and resource dependency chains. Use this agent for analyzing render pass graphs and pipeline dependencies. ([haolange/RDC-AgentCaps](https://github.com/haolange/RDC-AgentCaps/blob/HEAD/debugger/platforms/copilot-ide/agents/04_pass_graph_pipeline.agent.md) · ★15)
- **pass-graph-pipeline**: Internal specialist for pass and event divergence plus pipeline state localization. Use when narrowing the issue to a pass, drawcall, or event. ([haolange/RDC-AgentCaps](https://github.com/haolange/RDC-AgentCaps/blob/HEAD/debugger/platforms/claude-code/.claude/agents/04_pass_graph_pipeline.md) · ★15)
- **pass-intent**: Intent review pass — does the diff do what the PR title and body say it does? Body+title only; questions and nits, never blockers. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-intent.md) · ★9)
- **pass-lint-conformance**: Lint and format conformance (sub-lens of style) — planned only when a CI job of the repo runs a linter or formatter whose rules cover the changed files; reports only violations on changed lines that would fail that job. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-lint-conformance.md) · ★9)
- **pass-maintainability**: Maintainability review pass — will the next person reading or changing this code understand it and change it safely? Stale docs and comments, misleading names, dead code, undecodable values, weak types, hidden coupling. Never style. Capped at P2. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-maintainability.md) · ★9)
- **pass-placement**: Placement review pass — is this change in the right place? Judged against the repo's own structure; findings name the better home; capped at P2. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-placement.md) · ★9)
- **pass-prediction-engineer**: Implements deterministic ISS pass prediction, twilight and illumination gates, observer location handling, city lookup, and pass-card semantics. ([MertArtun/orbital](https://github.com/MertArtun/orbital/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pass-prediction-engineer.md))
- **pass-prod-risk**: Production-risk review pass — behavior changes to existing endpoints, rollout widening, cascade and latency risk. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-prod-risk.md) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **pass-rollout**: Mixed-version rollout pass (sub-lens of backcompat) — for releases that will not reach every party at once, checks old peer against new code, new peer against old code, and state persisted by the old version. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-rollout.md) · ★9)
- **pass-roundtrip**: Persisted-bytes round-trip pass (sub-lens of data-safety) — traces sample files through read, detect, decode, edit, encode and write, and checks the bytes that land on disk. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-roundtrip.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sec-access**: Object-access security pass — can this principal touch this object: IDOR and BOLA, the tenant key on every query, ordinary mass assignment, CSRF, CORS and framing, row-level security predicates; found by diffing each operation's object check against its siblings'. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sec-access.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sec-authn-coverage**: Authentication-coverage security pass — tabulates every in-scope entry point (HTTP, GraphQL, gRPC, WebSocket, webhook, cron target, server action, IaC- or backend-declared endpoint, ops/debug surface), resolves the guard chain that actually runs on it, and reports the ones an attacker reaches without the authentication or function-level authorization their siblings have, or through a check that does not stop the request. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sec-authn-coverage.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sec-boundaries**: Boundaries security pass — where input names a file or a URL: path traversal, archive extraction, uploads, SSRF (rebinding, redirects, key URLs), open redirect, XXE, file permissions and symlinks; canonicalise first, then check, against an allowlist. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sec-boundaries.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sec-exploit-chain**: Exploit-chain security lens — builds cited multi-hop attack chains to a goal no single hop reaches, from the starting point its task's strategy names (delta, forward, backward or compose). ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sec-exploit-chain.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sec-iam**: Platform-grant review pass (security profile) — resolves each cloud IAM, federation, Kubernetes RBAC, Vault, database-role or host grant the diff adds, widens or un-guards to the weakest actor it admits and the strongest identity it confers. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sec-iam.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sec-infra**: Security infrastructure lens — who can reach what, and whether every control on the way is in force. Host firewalls, security groups, load-balancer scheme, network policy, admission and policy engines, proxy paths and origin locks, tunnels, public storage, planes with auth switched off, node-credential reach, runtime privilege, and audit sinks a PR removes or mutes. A control counts only when every link of its chain is cited in the config the repo ships; live state is noted, never claimed. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sec-infra.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sec-injection**: Injection security pass — untrusted data read as code or query: SQL and NoSQL, OS command, server-side template, XSS, deserialization, eval, LDAP and XPath, header and log lines; one micro-rubric per sink, the framework's sanitizer located, every escape hatch named. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sec-injection.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sec-llm**: Model-integration security pass — untrusted text reaching a model's context, and model output reaching a sink or a tool with authority; every finding names the payload or untrusted source, the model hop and the control effect, each cited. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sec-llm.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sec-privesc**: Privilege-escalation review pass (security profile) — every application surface that grants, inherits, assumes or overrides authority (roles and role definitions, memberships and groups, scopes, ownership, impersonation, invites, SSO/SCIM role mapping, domain claims, integration consent, setup bootstrap, override inputs), takes over a higher account through its recovery channel, or weakens a security setting is traced from the weakest actor who can reach it to the power it confers; the role gate is checked for failing open, the target for tenant and rank, and a permitted grant for ceiling, ap… ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sec-privesc.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sec-secrets-data**: Secrets and data-exposure security pass — credential literals wherever they sit, secrets in logs, traces, responses and caches, personal data shown to the wrong principal, weak cryptography and randomness, and cache keys that ignore the tenant. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sec-secrets-data.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sec-supply-chain**: Supply-chain security pass — dependency adds and bumps, lockfile-only changes, install scripts and typosquats read from the package's source, CI workflows (privileged triggers, event text in run steps, unpinned actions, token scopes) and base images. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sec-supply-chain.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sec-toctou**: Security time-of-check / time-of-use lens — a security decision is checked on one state, object or representation and acted on another while the attacker acts in the gap. Parallel and batched limit overruns, single-use tokens redeemed twice, uniqueness and upserts that do not hold, check/use binding mismatches, provider-retried webhooks, privileged check-then-open, a CI gate followed by a mutable-ref checkout. Every finding carries a recipe and the serializer it hunted. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sec-toctou.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sec-tokens**: Token and credential lifecycle pass (security profile) — for every credential check that runs, asks whether the token it accepts can be forged, replayed, used for the wrong purpose or account, outlive its revocation or its key, or reach someone it was not issued to, and whether a login path, factor change or recovery channel gets past the factors it should require; walks issue → verify → consume → refresh → rotate → revoke → recover per token kind and hunts the defeating control before writing. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sec-tokens.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sec-variant**: Variant-analysis security pass — names the security pattern this pull request fixes or introduces, searches the repository for its unfixed or newly unsafe siblings, and reports only those sibling sites; in a scan, starts from the repository's past security fixes. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sec-variant.md) · ★9)
- **pass-security**: Security & tenancy review pass — injection, authz, tenant isolation, secrets in logs, cache safety. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-security.md) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **pass-simplicity**: Simplicity review pass — is the code as simple as its job allows, and do any of its checks defend states that cannot occur? May point beyond the diff at a simpler shape; capped at P2. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-simplicity.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sm-derived**: State-machine pass over derived state — finds stored values that copy or summarise other state (counts, totals, caches, mirrored flags, synced component state, a call replaced by a cached field) and a writer of the source that leaves them stale. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sm-derived.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sm-durable**: State-machine pass over stored state — finds state that outlives the process and breaks there, such as a persisted shape changed with no migration or version change, a state write and its side effect that are not atomic, sequential writes outside a transaction, a stored transition with no compare on the old state, recovery after a crash, and old rows, files or messages meeting new code. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sm-durable.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sm-events**: State-machine pass over events — finds handlers that break when an event arrives twice, late, out of order or while the previous one is still in flight, such as double submits, redelivered messages, stale responses, late callbacks, re-entrant calls. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sm-events.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sm-exhaustive**: State-machine pass over exhaustiveness — when a change adds, renames or removes a state, finds every place that enumerates the set and was not updated, such as dispatches, status lists, maps, filters and stored values. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sm-exhaustive.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sm-lifecycle**: State-machine pass over lifecycle order — finds APIs and objects that only work when their calls come in an order nothing enforces, such as use before init, use after close or dispose, missing or doubled teardown. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sm-lifecycle.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sm-states**: State-machine pass over representable illegal states — finds flag, nullable-field and status combinations that must never exist and that a writer in this change produces or a reader mishandles. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sm-states.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sm-stuck**: State-machine pass over stuck states — finds a transient state (loading, processing, locked, in progress, a held reference) that an error, early return, timeout or cancel path leaves set forever, or that a reset moved into one branch no longer leaves. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sm-stuck.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sm-transitions**: State-machine pass over transitions — finds writes of a status or lifecycle state that do not check the state they leave, guards on the wrong state, and transitions that skip the work their siblings do. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sm-transitions.md) · ★9)
- **pass-statelessness**: Statelessness review pass — in-process state that breaks under many pods and rolling deploys. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-statelessness.md) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **pass-style**: Style review pass — only where the repo writes its code style down. Findings cite the guide's own rule and the changed line that breaks it; never taste, never another ecosystem's conventions, never what a linter or formatter enforces. Nit only. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-style.md) · ★9)
- **pass-survey**: The setup survey's lens — reads one survey case of the reviewed repo (the repo-wide directive files and design docs, or one unit's code sample) and writes the conventions and architecture a reviewer must know. Hunts no bugs. Launched by the setup engine; not for ad-hoc questions (use explore). ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/docker/companion/agents/pass-survey.md) · ★9)
- **pass-sweep**: Line-by-line sweep of a few changed files for local slips — typos, fall-through, a copied line not updated, shipped placeholders, unchecked parsing — the defects a whole-PR reading skims past. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-sweep.md) · ★9)
- **pass-tests**: Tests review pass — which existing tests, mocks and fixtures the change breaks, and whether the PR's tests actually test the code or mock away the failure point. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-tests.md) · ★9)
- **pass-ui**: UI-state review pass — frontend effects, mutations, translations, tokens, async pipeline durability. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-ui.md) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **pass-validate**: Adversarial validation pass — every candidate finding is re-derived from the code before posting; what does not hold up is doubted or killed. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-validate.md) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **pass-widened**: Widened-check review pass (sub-lens of baseline) — when a predicate, guard or accepted-value list changes, every consumer that trusted the old set is checked against each newly admitted member. ([quickchatai/quickreview](https://github.com/quickchatai/quickreview/blob/HEAD/agents/pass-widened.md) · ★9)
- **pass1-structural-rewriter**: You are Pass 1 of a text-to-speech rewrite pipeline. You do the judgment-heavy STRUCTURAL work only and leave the rest for a later pass. Read `rules_reference` first, in full — it defines your scope and what to leave untouched for Pass 2. Follow it exactly; do not step into token-level work. ([ArunskiOrg/BenArunskiUtils](https://github.com/ArunskiOrg/BenArunskiUtils/blob/HEAD/skills/tts/agents/pass1-structural-rewriter.md) · ★1)
- **pass2-token-sweep**: You are Pass 2 of a text-to-speech rewrite pipeline.
